title: Resumed original scope family passes 2698 assertions; original serving study frozen and unrun

All four original scope native cases pass on frozen V304: 812 assertions at 2,051 tokens, 812 at 4,096 tokens, 832 lifecycle assertions and 242 MTP/image rollback assertions, totaling 2,698. This is correctness evidence for this exact source family, not final V360 composition, real-photo quality, preview speed or sustained throughput. Original 1,800-second work and 30-second cleanup allowances, 20.112 GB startup, 17.112 GB owned and 3 GB live headroom remain unchanged. Earlier V331 pressure failure and V364 thermal admission refusals are preserved. All owned groups are independently empty and the model lock is free at 14:05:44 UTC. The original V170 32-cell serving study is frozen with unchanged workloads, controls and acceptance, but is unrun at capture; its benchmark window still needs coordination after other heavy tasks drain. User apps remain open. No build, default activation, installation or release is performed.

````````````text
    BASE / 'current-scope-qualification-v331/run.py',
    BASE / 'current-scope-qualification-v331/preparation.json',
    ROOT / '.build/optimization/current-scope-qualification-v331/native/family-2051/attempt.json',
    ROOT / '.build/optimization/current-scope-qualification-v331/native/family-2051/qualification.json',
    ROOT / '.build/optimization/current-scope-qualification-v331/native/family-2051/stdout.txt',
    ROOT / '.build/optimization/current-scope-qualification-v331/native/family-2051/stderr.txt',
    BASE / 'pressure-footprint-v341/guard_with_footprint.py',
    BASE / 'pressure-footprint-v341/pressure_footprint.py',
]
ENV = {'SLOTSTREAM_OPT_WORKSPACE_TILE': '1024', 'SLOTSTREAM_OPT_SCOPE_FRONTIER': '1'}
NATIVE = {
    'family-2051': {'variant': 'scope-integrated-family', 'tokens': 2051, 'report': 'optimization-256-compute-read-scope', 'minimum_assertions': 117},
    'family-4096': {'variant': 'scope-integrated-family', 'tokens': 4096, 'report': 'optimization-256-compute-read-scope', 'minimum_assertions': 117},
    'lifecycle': {'variant': 'scope-integrated-lifecycle', 'tokens': None, 'report': 'optimization-scope-lifecycle', 'minimum_assertions': 832},
    'mtp-vision': {'variant': 'scope-integrated-mtp-vision', 'tokens': None, 'report': 'optimization-scope-mtp-vision', 'minimum_assertions': 240},
}
NATIVE_SECONDS, SERVING_SECONDS = 1800, 5400
STARTUP_GB = 20.112
NATIVE_OWNED_BYTES = 17_112_000_000


def commands(value):
    model = c.read(DRAFT)['model']
    result = {}
    for name, item in NATIVE.items():
        arguments = [value['build']['binary'], 'optimization-state-check', '--variant', item['variant']]
        if item['tokens'] is not None:
            arguments += ['--tokens', str(item['tokens'])]
        arguments += ['--model', model, '--json']
        # The common executor removes inherited experiment controls. env adds
        # only the two explicitly frozen geometry controls to this child.
        result[name] = ['/usr/bin/env'] + [key + '=' + val for key, val in ENV.items()] + arguments
    return result


def prepare():
    require(not (HERE / 'preparation.json').exists() and not OUT.exists(), 'already prepared or attempted')
    value = core.load()
    original = c.read(DRAFT)
    require(original['binary'] == 'FINAL_BINARY_REQUIRED' and original['rounds'] == 16
            and original['max_tokens'] == 16 and original['memory_gb'] == 10, 'original scope draft differs')
    # serve_bench adds per-arm scope/workspace headroom to its13GB base.
    scope = original['arms']['scope']
    require(core.c.bench.measurement_memory(original) == 13
            and scope['chunk'] == 256 and scope['env']['SLOTSTREAM_OPT_LAYER_WORKSPACE'] == '1'
            and scope['env']['SLOTSTREAM_OPT_READ_SCOPE'] == '4096'
            and abs(13 + 2 + (4096-256)*1.30e-3 + .12 - STARTUP_GB) < 1e-9,
            'original scope geometry allowance differs')
    result = {'format': 'slotstream-current-scope-qualification-v1', 'prepared_at': core.now(),
              'build': value['build'], 'shared_proof': value['shared_proof'], 'native': NATIVE,
              'native_environment': ENV, 'native_commands': commands(value),
              'native_seconds': NATIVE_SECONDS, 'serving_seconds': SERVING_SECONDS,
              'startup_reclaimable_gb': STARTUP_GB, 'native_owned_limit_bytes': NATIVE_OWNED_BYTES,
              'executor_sha256': digest(Path(__file__)), 'core_executor_sha256': digest(CORE),
              'core_preparation_sha256': digest(core.HERE / 'preparation.json'),
              'resumption_sha256': {str(p): digest(p) for p in RESUMPTION},
              'original_draft_sha256': digest(DRAFT), 'fixture_sha256': original['fixture_sha256'],
              'classification': 'New prospective native family after the user resumed and resources changed. Original V331 family2051 pressure interruption is preserved; no earlier report or timing is relabeled. Exact frozen V304, all four original native cases and unchanged original V170 serving controls remain. V341 additive failure observer is explicitly bound. No model run or automatic adoption at preparation.'}
    c.write_new(HERE / 'preparation.json', result)
    return {'prepared': True, 'native_unrun': list(NATIVE), 'serving_unrun': True}


def load():
    value, shared = c.read(HERE / 'preparation.json'), core.load()
    require(value['resumption_sha256'] == {str(p): digest(p) for p in RESUMPTION}, 'resumption evidence or observer changed')
    require(value['executor_sha256'] == digest(Path(__file__)) and value['core_executor_sha256'] == digest(CORE)
            and value['core_preparation_sha256'] == digest(core.HERE / 'preparation.json'), 'executor/core identity changed')
    require(value['original_draft_sha256'] == digest(DRAFT)
            and value['fixture_sha256'] == digest(Path(c.read(DRAFT)['fixture'])), 'original scope input changed')
    require(value['build'] == shared['build'] and value['shared_proof'] == shared['shared_proof'], 'current source/build/native identity changed')
    require(value['native'] == NATIVE and value['native_environment'] == ENV and value['native_commands'] == commands(shared)
            and value['native_seconds'] == NATIVE_SECONDS and value['serving_seconds'] == SERVING_SECONDS
            and value['startup_reclaimable_gb'] == STARTUP_GB and value['native_owned_limit_bytes'] == NATIVE_OWNED_BYTES,
            'scope workload, geometry or allowance changed')
    return value


def assess_native_report(name, report):
    item = NATIVE[name]
    require(report.get('name') == item['report'] and report.get('passed') is True and report.get('skipped') is None
            and len(report.get('items', [])) >= item['minimum_assertions']
            and all(row.get('passed') is True for row in report['items']), 'required scope native assertions failed or missing')
    measured = report.get('measurements', {})
    require(measured.get('integrated_base') == 1, 'scope did not run the integrated base')
    if item['tokens'] is not None:
        require(measured.get('workspace_token_tile') == 1024 and measured.get('compact_scope_frontier') == 1
                and [measured.get(f'arm{n}.chunk') for n in range(3)] == [256, 512, min(4096, item['tokens'])],
                'native family geometry or compute schedules differ')
    return len(report['items'])


def finalize(target, result):
    """The outer receipt also binds this adapter after the common cleanup."""
    try:
        load()
        result['scope_proofs_unchanged'] = True
    except BaseException as error:
        result['scope_proof_error'] = str(error)
        for key in ('passed', 'completed', 'qualified'):
            result[key] = False
    result['execution_receipt_sha256'] = digest(target / 'receipt.json')
    c.write_new(target / 'qualification.json', result)
    return result


def native(name, deadline):
    require(name in NATIVE, 'unknown scope native prerequisite')
    core.allowance(deadline, NATIVE_SECONDS + 30)
    value = load()
    target = OUT / 'native' / name
    require(not target.exists(), 'attempted scope native cannot retry')
    limit, before, thermal = core.readiness(deadline, NATIVE_SECONDS + 30, STARTUP_GB)
    policy = dict(c.NATIVE_POLICY, startup_reclaimable_bytes=int(STARTUP_GB * 1e9),
                  maximum_owned_rss_bytes=NATIVE_OWNED_BYTES, maximum_build_seconds=NATIVE_SECONDS)
    command = value['native_commands'][name]
    attempt = {'started_at': core.now(), 'deadline_utc': deadline, 'command': command, 'environment': ENV,
               'build': value['build'], 'before': before, 'thermal_prelaunch': thermal, 'policy': policy,
               'preparation_sha256': digest(HERE / 'preparation.json'),
               'classification': 'Fresh prospective V364 after the preserved V331 pressure interruption and later resource change. Original native numerical/state/route/rollback workload on exact V304;640slots, native-owned multiple comparison states and original1024workspace/compactfrontier geometry.20.112GB startup envelope,3GB live headroom. This is not serving capacity or timing evidence.'}
    def assess(code):
        load()
        require(code == 0, 'scope native process failed')
        count = assess_native_report(name, c.read(target / 'stdout.txt'))
        return {'completed': True, 'passed': True, 'qualified': True, 'assertions': count}
    return finalize(target, core.execute(command, target, NATIVE_SECONDS, policy, assess, limit, attempt))


def prerequisites():
    value = load()
    result = {}
    for name in NATIVE:
        target = OUT / 'native' / name
        report, receipt, attempt = (c.read(target / file) for file in ('stdout.txt', 'qualification.json', 'attempt.json'))
        require(receipt.get('passed') is True and receipt.get('cleanup_complete') is True
                and receipt.get('within_reservation') is True and receipt.get('scope_proofs_unchanged') is True,
                'scope prerequisite has not passed and drained')
        require(receipt['stdout_sha256'] == digest(target / 'stdout.txt')
                and receipt['attempt_sha256'] == digest(target / 'attempt.json')
                and receipt['execution_receipt_sha256'] == digest(target / 'receipt.json')
                and attempt['command'] == value['native_commands'][name] and attempt['environment'] == ENV
                and attempt['build'] == value['build'], 'scope native evidence or identity changed')
        result[name] = {'assertions': assess_native_report(name, report),
                        'evidence': c.evidence(target, ['attempt.json', 'receipt.json', 'qualification.json', 'stdout.txt', 'stderr.txt'])}
    return result


def materialize(value):
    protocol = copy.deepcopy(c.read(DRAFT))
    protocol['binary'] = value['build']['binary']
    for arm in protocol['arms'].values():
        require(arm['binary'] == 'FINAL_BINARY_REQUIRED', 'scope arm already bound')
        arm['binary'] = value['build']['binary']
    protocol['label'] = 'resumed-scope-long-prefill-v364'
    protocol['classification'] = 'Prospective unchanged original V170 fixed long-prefill study; exact V304 and current scope native prerequisites. No adoption.'
    del protocol['candidate_prerequisites_pending']
    return protocol


def freeze_serving():
    value, proof = load(), prerequisites()
    require(not (HERE / 'protocol.json').exists() and not (OUT / 'serving').exists(), 'scope serving already frozen or attempted')
    c.write_new(HERE / 'protocol.json', materialize(value))
    c.write_new(HERE / 'binding.json', {'prepared_at': core.now(), 'preparation_sha256': digest(HERE / 'preparation.json'),
                                      'protocol_sha256': digest(HERE / 'protocol.json'), 'native_proof': proof})
    return {'frozen': True, 'protocol_sha256': digest(HERE / 'protocol.json'), 'model_execution': False}


def load_serving():
    value, protocol, binding = load(), c.read(HERE / 'protocol.json'), c.read(HERE / 'binding.json')
    require(protocol == materialize(value) and binding['preparation_sha256'] == digest(HERE / 'preparation.json')
            and binding['protocol_sha256'] == digest(HERE / 'protocol.json')
            and binding['native_proof'] == prerequisites(), 'scope serving workload, criteria or proof changed')
    return value, protocol


def assess_serving():
    value, protocol = load_serving()
    output = OUT / 'serving'
    manifest, completion = c.read(output / 'manifest.json'), c.read(output / 'completion.json')
    require(manifest['protocol'] == protocol and manifest['arm_builds'] == {a: value['build'] for a in protocol['arms']}, 'executed scope identity differs')
    require(completion.get('interrupted') is False and completion.get('stopped_early') is False and 'error' not in completion
            and completion.get('planned_cells') == completion.get('recorded_cells') == 32, 'scope fixed study incomplete')
    rows = [json.loads(line) for line in (output / 'results.jsonl').read_text().splitlines()]
    require(len(rows) == 32 and {(r['round'], r['arm']) for r in rows} == {(n, a) for n in range(1, 17) for a in protocol['arms']},
            'missing, extra or repeated scope cells')
    warmups = {(r['round'], r['arm']): c.read(output / f"{r['round']}-{r['arm']}/warmup.json") for r in rows}
    c.soak.validate_paired_execution('scope-long-prefill', protocol, rows, warmups)
    for row in rows:
        c.bench.validate_work_observation(protocol['work_constraints'], row['arm'], row['metrics']['stats'])
    summary = c.bench.summaries(rows, 'reference', protocol['comparison_basis'], protocol['required_equal_work'])
    acceptance = c.bench.acceptance_results(summary, protocol['acceptance'])
    startup = c.bench.startup_acceptance_results(rows, 'reference', protocol['startup_acceptance'])
    require(acceptance == completion['acceptance'] and startup == completion['startup_acceptance'], 'scope recorded acceptance differs from recomputation')
    qualified = bool(acceptance and startup and all(x['passed'] is True for x in acceptance + startup))
    return {'completed': True, 'passed': qualified, 'qualified': qualified, 'cells': 32, 'acceptance': acceptance,
            'startup_acceptance': startup, 'summary': summary, 'activation_performed': False}


def serving(deadline):
    core.allowance(deadline, SERVING_SECONDS + 60)
    value, protocol = load_serving()
    target, output = HERE / 'execution', OUT / 'serving'
    require(not target.exists() and not output.exists(), 'attempted scope serving cannot retry')
    limit, before, thermal = core.readiness(deadline, SERVING_SECONDS + 60, STARTUP_GB)
    policy = dict(c.NATIVE_POLICY, startup_reclaimable_bytes=int(STARTUP_GB * 1e9),
                  maximum_owned_rss_bytes=12_000_000_000, maximum_build_seconds=SERVING_SECONDS)
    command = [sys.executable, str(ROOT / 'Tools/serve_bench.py'), '--protocol', str(HERE / 'protocol.json'), '--out', str(output)]
    attempt = {'started_at': core.now(), 'deadline_utc': deadline, 'command': command, 'environment': {},
               'build': value['build'], 'before': before, 'thermal_prelaunch': thermal, 'policy': policy,
               'protocol_sha256': digest(HERE / 'protocol.json'),
               'classification': 'One original32-cell V170 long-prefill study. Native10GB request/warmup cap;12GB owned tree.20.112GB original scope admission. No replacement, shortening, prior pooling or activation.'}
    def assess(code):
        require(code in (0, 1), 'scope serving process failed')
        return assess_serving()
    result = finalize(target, core.execute(command, target, SERVING_SECONDS, policy, assess, limit, attempt, output / 'results.jsonl'))
    if output.exists():
        c.write_new(target / 'artifact-sha256.json', c.artifact_hashes(output))
    return result


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('command', choices=['prepare', 'status', 'native', 'freeze-serving', 'serving'])
    parser.add_argument('--name', choices=list(NATIVE))
    parser.add_argument('--deadline')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    with (HERE / '.run.lock').open('a') as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
        if args.command == 'prepare':
            result = prepare()
        elif args.command == 'status':
            load()
            result = {'native': {n: 'attempted' if (OUT / 'native' / n).exists() else 'unrun' for n in NATIVE},
                      'serving': 'attempted' if (HERE / 'execution').exists() else 'unrun'}
        elif args.command == 'freeze-serving':
            result = freeze_serving()
        else:
            require(args.deadline is not None, 'peer-granted deadline required')
            result = native(args.name, args.deadline) if args.command == 'native' else serving(args.deadline)
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2), flush=True)
    return 0 if args.command not in ('native', 'serving') or result.get('completed') else 1


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
````````````

````````````text
"""No-model tests of the original scope workload and adapter proof boundary."""
import copy
import datetime
import json
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch
import run as subject


class Gates(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.value = subject.load()
        self.future = (datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc) + datetime.timedelta(hours=3)).isoformat()

    def test_exact_original_serving_work_and_native_geometry(self):
        original = subject.c.read(subject.DRAFT)
        bound = subject.materialize(self.value)
        metadata = {'binary', 'arms', 'classification', 'label', 'candidate_prerequisites_pending'}
        self.assertEqual({k: v for k, v in original.items() if k not in metadata},
                         {k: v for k, v in bound.items() if k not in metadata})
        for arm in original['arms']:
            self.assertEqual(len(bound['arms'][arm]['env']), 54)
            self.assertEqual({k: v for k, v in original['arms'][arm].items() if k != 'binary'},
                             {k: v for k, v in bound['arms'][arm].items() if k != 'binary'})
        self.assertEqual(subject.ENV, {'SLOTSTREAM_OPT_WORKSPACE_TILE': '1024', 'SLOTSTREAM_OPT_SCOPE_FRONTIER': '1'})
        for name, command in self.value['native_commands'].items():
            self.assertEqual(command[:4], ['/usr/bin/env', 'SLOTSTREAM_OPT_WORKSPACE_TILE=1024',
                                           'SLOTSTREAM_OPT_SCOPE_FRONTIER=1', self.value['build']['binary']])
            item = subject.NATIVE[name]
            self.assertEqual(command[command.index('--variant')+1], item['variant'])
            if item['tokens'] is not None:
                self.assertEqual(command[command.index('--tokens')+1], str(item['tokens']))
            else:
                self.assertNotIn('--tokens', command)
        self.assertFalse(subject.OUT.exists())

    def test_expired_short_unzoned_deadline_cannot_claim_any_work(self):
        for deadline in ['2026-09-07T21:00:00Z', datetime.datetime.now().isoformat(),
                         (datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)+datetime.timedelta(seconds=1700)).isoformat()]:
            with self.subTest(deadline=deadline), patch.object(subject.core, 'guarded_run') as launch:
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    subject.native('family-2051', deadline)
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    subject.serving(deadline)
                launch.assert_not_called()
        self.assertFalse(subject.OUT.exists())

    def test_native_admission_keeps_the_full_scope_envelope(self):
        with patch.object(subject.core, 'readiness', side_effect=RuntimeError('synthetic unavailable interval')) as readiness, \
                patch.object(subject.core, 'guarded_run') as launch:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, 'synthetic'):
                subject.native('family-4096', self.future)
            readiness.assert_called_once_with(self.future, 1830, 20.112)
            launch.assert_not_called()

    def test_missing_prerequisites_and_prior_attempts_cannot_launch(self):
        with patch.object(subject.core, 'guarded_run') as launch:
            with self.assertRaises(FileNotFoundError):
                subject.freeze_serving()
            launch.assert_not_called()
        self.assertFalse((subject.HERE / 'protocol.json').exists())
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            out = Path(directory)
            (out / 'native/family-2051').mkdir(parents=True)
            with patch.object(subject, 'OUT', out), patch.object(subject.core, 'guarded_run') as launch:
                with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'cannot retry'):
                    subject.native('family-2051', self.future)
                launch.assert_not_called()

    def test_native_report_requires_actual_integrated_geometry_and_every_assertion(self):
        item = subject.NATIVE['family-2051']
        report = {'name': item['report'], 'passed': True,
                  'items': [{'passed': True} for _ in range(item['minimum_assertions'])],
                  'measurements': {'integrated_base': 1, 'workspace_token_tile': 1024,
                                   'compact_scope_frontier': 1, 'arm0.chunk': 256, 'arm1.chunk': 512, 'arm2.chunk': 2051}}
        self.assertEqual(subject.assess_native_report('family-2051', report), 117)
        for key, val in [('integrated_base', 0), ('workspace_token_tile', 256), ('compact_scope_frontier', 0), ('arm2.chunk', 4096)]:
            altered = copy.deepcopy(report)
            altered['measurements'][key] = val
            with self.subTest(key=key), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                subject.assess_native_report('family-2051', altered)
        for alteration in ['failed_item', 'missing_item', 'skip', 'wrong_report']:
            altered = copy.deepcopy(report)
            if alteration == 'failed_item':
                altered['items'][-1]['passed'] = False
            elif alteration == 'missing_item':
                altered['items'].pop()
            elif alteration == 'skip':
                altered['skipped'] = 'unavailable'
            else:
                altered['name'] = 'different-diagnostic'
            with self.subTest(alteration=alteration), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                subject.assess_native_report('family-2051', altered)

    def test_changed_scope_proof_cannot_inherit_successful_common_cleanup(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            target = Path(directory)
            (target / 'receipt.json').write_text('{"synthetic_common_cleanup":true}\n')
            report = {'passed': True, 'completed': True, 'qualified': True, 'cleanup_complete': True}
            with patch.object(subject, 'load', side_effect=ValueError('synthetic changed scope proof')):
                result = subject.finalize(target, report)
            self.assertFalse(result['passed'])
            self.assertFalse(result['completed'])
            self.assertFalse(result['qualified'])
            self.assertIn('changed scope proof', result['scope_proof_error'])
            self.assertEqual(json.loads((target / 'qualification.json').read_text()), result)

    def test_original_failure_and_observer_bytes_are_bound(self):
        original = subject.digest
        for changed in subject.RESUMPTION:
            with self.subTest(changed=str(changed)), patch.object(subject, 'digest',
                    side_effect=lambda path: '0'*64 if Path(path) == changed else original(path)):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    subject.load()
        previous = subject.c.read(subject.ROOT / '.build/optimization/current-scope-qualification-v331/native/family-2051/qualification.json')
        self.assertFalse(previous['qualified'])
        self.assertEqual(subject.STARTUP_GB, 20.112)
        self.assertEqual(subject.NATIVE_OWNED_BYTES, 17_112_000_000)

    def test_scope_core_draft_and_fixture_identities_are_frozen(self):
        original = subject.digest
        paths = [Path(subject.__file__), subject.CORE, subject.core.HERE / 'preparation.json',
                 subject.DRAFT, Path(subject.c.read(subject.DRAFT)['fixture'])]
        for changed in paths:
            with self.subTest(changed=str(changed)), patch.object(subject, 'digest',
                    side_effect=lambda path: '0'*64 if Path(path) == changed else original(path)):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    subject.load()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    start = time.monotonic()
    result = unittest.TextTestRunner(verbosity=2).run(unittest.defaultTestLoader.loadTestsFromTestCase(Gates))
    value = {'passed': result.wasSuccessful(), 'methods': result.testsRun, 'elapsed_seconds': time.monotonic()-start,
             'model_output_created': subject.OUT.exists(), 'serving_protocol_created': (subject.HERE / 'protocol.json').exists(),
             'executor_sha256': subject.digest(Path(subject.__file__)), 'tests_sha256': subject.digest(Path(__file__)),
             'preparation_sha256': subject.digest(subject.HERE / 'preparation.json')}
    subject.c.write_new(subject.HERE / 'validation.json', value)
    print(json.dumps(value), flush=True)
    raise SystemExit(0 if result.wasSuccessful() else 1)
````````````
